101 // if constexpr is C++17+; C++14 has relaxed constexpr but not if constexpr.
102 #if __cplusplus >= 201703L
103 #define LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR LUMEX_CONSTEXPR
104 #elif __cplusplus >= 201402L
105 #define LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR LUMEX_CONSTEXPR
106 #else
107 #define LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR inline
108 #endif
109 
110 // std::array::at() is not constexpr until C++17; constexpr lookup tables need
111 // operator[] on C++11/14.
112 #if __cplusplus >= 201703L
113 #define LUMEX_CRC_ARRAY_REF(table, index) \
114  (table).at (static_cast<std::size_t> (index))
115 #else
116 #define LUMEX_CRC_ARRAY_REF(table, index) \
117  (table)[static_cast<std::size_t> (index)]
118 #endif

120 LUMEX_CONST_NUM int kMaxCrcBitWidth = 64;
121 LUMEX_CONST_NUM int kBitsPerByte = 8;
122 LUMEX_CONST_NUM std::size_t kTableByteCount = 256U;
123 LUMEX_CONST_NUM std::uint8_t kByteMaskU8 = 0xFFU;
124 
125 #if __cplusplus >= 201402L
126 template <typename Spec>
127 LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_FUNCTION void
128 ValidateSpec () LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
129 {
130  LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (Spec::kWidth > 0 && Spec::kWidth <= kMaxCrcBitWidth,
131  "Spec::kWidth must be 1..64");
132  LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (
133  std::numeric_limits<typename Spec::ValueType>::is_integer,
134  "Spec::ValueType must be an integer type");
135  LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (
136  std::numeric_limits<typename Spec::ValueType>::digits >= Spec::kWidth,
137  "Spec::ValueType must hold at least kWidth bits");
138 }
139 #else
140 template <typename Spec>
141 inline void
142 ValidateSpec () LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
143 {
144  struct validator_t
145  {
146  LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (Spec::kWidth > 0
147  && Spec::kWidth <= kMaxCrcBitWidth,
148  "Spec::kWidth must be 1..64");
149  LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (
150  std::numeric_limits<typename Spec::ValueType>::is_integer,
151  "Spec::ValueType must be an integer type");
152  LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (
153  std::numeric_limits<typename Spec::ValueType>::digits >= Spec::kWidth,
154  "Spec::ValueType must hold at least kWidth bits");
155  };
156  (void)sizeof (validator_t);
157 }
158 #endif
159 
160 // Partial specialization: avoid (1u64 << Width) when Width == digits(uint64_t)
161 // (UB / -Wshift).
162 template <int Width, typename Enable = void> struct mask_impl_t
163 {
164  static LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR std::uint64_t
165  Value () LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
166  {
167  return (std::uint64_t{ 1 } << static_cast<unsigned> (Width))
168  - std::uint64_t{ 1 };
169  }
170 };
171 
172 template <int Width>
173 struct mask_impl_t<
174  Width,
175  typename std::enable_if<
176  Width == std::numeric_limits<std::uint64_t>::digits, void>::type>
177 {
178  static LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR std::uint64_t
179  Value () LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
180  {
181  return ~std::uint64_t{};
182  }
183 };
184 
185 template <int Width>
186 LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR std::uint64_t
187 Mask () LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
188 {
189  LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (Width > 0 && Width <= kMaxCrcBitWidth,
190  "CRC width must be in 1..64");
191  return mask_impl_t<Width>::Value ();
192 }
193 
194 // NOLINTBEGIN(bugprone-easily-swappable-parameters) - signatures match RevEng
195 // notation (data, width / index, poly)
196 LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR std::uint64_t
197 Reflect (std::uint64_t data, int width) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
198 {
199  std::uint64_t reflected = 0;
200  for (int bitIndex = 0; bitIndex < width; ++bitIndex)
201  if (((data >> bitIndex) & 1U) != 0U)
202  reflected |= (std::uint64_t{ 1 } << (width - 1 - bitIndex));
203  return reflected;
204 }
205 
206 template <int Width>
207 LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR std::uint64_t
208 MsbTableByte (std::uint64_t tableIndex, std::uint64_t poly) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
209 {
210  LUMEX_STATIC_ASSERT_MSG (Width >= kBitsPerByte,
211  "MSB table entries assume width >= 8");
212  std::uint64_t const mask = Mask<Width> ();
213  std::uint64_t const highBitMask = std::uint64_t{ 1 } << (Width - 1);
214  std::uint64_t registerValue = (tableIndex << (Width - kBitsPerByte)) & mask;
215  for (int step = 0; step < kBitsPerByte; ++step)
216  if ((registerValue & highBitMask) != 0U)
217  registerValue = ((registerValue << 1) & mask) ^ poly;
218  else
219  registerValue = (registerValue << 1) & mask;
220  return registerValue & mask;
221 }
222 
223 template <int Width>
224 LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR std::uint64_t
225 LsbTableByte (std::uint64_t tableIndex, std::uint64_t poly) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
226 {
227  std::uint64_t const mask = Mask<Width> ();
228  std::uint64_t const reflectedPoly = Reflect (poly, Width) & mask;
229  std::uint64_t registerValue = tableIndex;
230  for (int step = 0; step < kBitsPerByte; ++step)
231  if ((registerValue & 1U) != 0U)
232  registerValue = (registerValue >> 1) ^ reflectedPoly;
233  else
234  registerValue >>= 1;
235  return registerValue & mask;
236 }
237 
238 // NOLINTEND(bugprone-easily-swappable-parameters)
239 
240 // C++14: non-const std::array::operator[]/at are not constexpr; build the
241 // table via index_sequence
242 // and aggregate initialization instead of a mutating loop inside
243 // constexpr MakeLookupTable.
244 #if __cplusplus >= 201402L
245 template <typename Spec, std::size_t Index>
246 LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_FUNCTION std::uint64_t
247 LookupTableEntry () LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
248 {
249  return Spec::kRefIn
250  ? LsbTableByte<Spec::kWidth> (static_cast<std::uint64_t> (Index),
251  Spec::kPoly)
252  : MsbTableByte<Spec::kWidth> (static_cast<std::uint64_t> (Index),
253  Spec::kPoly);
254 }
255 
256 template <typename Spec, std::size_t... I>
257 LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_FUNCTION std::array<std::uint64_t, kTableByteCount>
258 MakeLookupTableImpl (std::index_sequence<I...>) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
259 {
260  return std::array<std::uint64_t, kTableByteCount>{
261  { LookupTableEntry<Spec, I> ()... }
262  };
263 }
264 #endif
265 
266 template <typename Spec>
267 LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR std::array<std::uint64_t, kTableByteCount>
268 MakeLookupTable () LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
269 {
270  ValidateSpec<Spec> ();
271 #if __cplusplus >= 201402L
272  return MakeLookupTableImpl<Spec> (
273  std::make_index_sequence<kTableByteCount>{});
274 #else
275  std::array<std::uint64_t, kTableByteCount> lookupTable{};
276  for (int tableIndex = 0; tableIndex < static_cast<int> (kTableByteCount);
277  ++tableIndex)
278  {
279  std::size_t const entry = static_cast<std::size_t> (tableIndex);
280  LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_IF (Spec::kRefIn)
281  LUMEX_CRC_ARRAY_REF (lookupTable, entry) = LsbTableByte<Spec::kWidth> (
282  static_cast<std::uint64_t> (tableIndex), Spec::kPoly);
283  else LUMEX_CRC_ARRAY_REF (lookupTable, entry)
284  = MsbTableByte<Spec::kWidth> (
285  static_cast<std::uint64_t> (tableIndex), Spec::kPoly);
286  }
287  return lookupTable;
288 #endif
289 }
290 
291 template <typename Spec>
292 LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR typename Spec::ValueType
293 ComputeBitwise (std::uint8_t const *data, std::size_t size) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
294 {
295  ValidateSpec<Spec> ();
296  std::uint64_t const mask = Mask<Spec::kWidth> ();
297  std::uint64_t const polyMasked = Spec::kPoly & mask;
298  std::uint64_t const polyReflected
299  = Reflect (polyMasked, Spec::kWidth) & mask;
300  std::uint64_t crcRegister
301  = (Spec::kRefIn ? Reflect (Spec::kInit, Spec::kWidth) : Spec::kInit)
302  & mask;
303 
304  if (data == nullptr || size == 0UL)
305  return static_cast<typename Spec::ValueType> (0);
306 
307  // NOLINTBEGIN(cppcoreguidelines-pro-bounds-pointer-arithmetic)
308  for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < size; ++offset)
309  {
310  std::uint8_t const messageByte = data[offset];
311  LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_IF (Spec::kRefIn)
312  {
313  for (int bitIndex = 0; bitIndex < kBitsPerByte; ++bitIndex)
314  {
315  std::uint8_t const dataBit = static_cast<std::uint8_t> (
316  (static_cast<unsigned> (messageByte) >> bitIndex) & 1U);
317  crcRegister ^= static_cast<std::uint64_t> (dataBit);
318  if ((crcRegister & 1U) != 0U)
319  crcRegister = (crcRegister >> 1) ^ polyReflected;
320  else
321  crcRegister >>= 1;
322  crcRegister &= mask;
323  }
324  }
325  else
326  {
327  int const msbShiftIndex = Spec::kWidth - 1;
328  for (int bitIndex = 0; bitIndex < kBitsPerByte; ++bitIndex)
329  {
330  std::uint8_t const dataBit = static_cast<std::uint8_t> (
331  (static_cast<unsigned> (messageByte)
332  >> (kBitsPerByte - 1 - bitIndex))
333  & 1U);
334  std::uint64_t const topBit = (crcRegister >> msbShiftIndex) & 1U;
335  crcRegister = (crcRegister << 1) & mask;
336  if ((topBit ^ static_cast<std::uint64_t> (dataBit)) != 0U)
337  crcRegister ^= polyMasked;
338  }
339  }
340  }
341  // NOLINTEND(cppcoreguidelines-pro-bounds-pointer-arithmetic)
342 
343  // RefIn=true: same as zlib / RevEng tables - no final Reflect before xorout.
344  LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_IF (!Spec::kRefIn && Spec::kRefOut)
345  crcRegister = Reflect (crcRegister, Spec::kWidth) & mask;
346  crcRegister = (crcRegister ^ Spec::kXorOut) & mask;
347  return static_cast<typename Spec::ValueType> (crcRegister);
348 }
349 
350 template <typename Spec>
351 LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR typename Spec::ValueType
352 ComputeTableDriven (std::uint8_t const *data, std::size_t size) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
353 {
354  ValidateSpec<Spec> ();
355 #if __cplusplus >= 201402L
356  LUMEX_CONSTEXPR std::array<std::uint64_t, kTableByteCount> kByteLookupTable
357  = MakeLookupTable<Spec> ();
358 #else
359  static std::array<std::uint64_t, kTableByteCount> const kByteLookupTable
360  = MakeLookupTable<Spec> ();
361 #endif
362  std::uint64_t const mask = Mask<Spec::kWidth> ();
363  std::uint64_t crcRegister
364  = (Spec::kRefIn ? Reflect (Spec::kInit, Spec::kWidth) : Spec::kInit)
365  & mask;
366 
367  if (data == nullptr || size == 0UL)
368  return static_cast<typename Spec::ValueType> (0);
369 
370  // NOLINTBEGIN(cppcoreguidelines-pro-bounds-pointer-arithmetic)
371  LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_IF (Spec::kRefIn)
372  {
373  for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < size; ++offset)
374  {
375  std::uint8_t const messageByte = data[offset];
376  crcRegister
377  = (crcRegister >> kBitsPerByte)
378  ^ LUMEX_CRC_ARRAY_REF (
379  kByteLookupTable,
380  (crcRegister ^ static_cast<std::uint64_t> (messageByte))
381  & kByteMaskU8);
382  crcRegister &= mask;
383  }
384  }
385  else
386  {
387  for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < size; ++offset)
388  {
389  std::uint8_t const messageByte = data[offset];
390  crcRegister = ((crcRegister << kBitsPerByte) & mask)
391  ^ LUMEX_CRC_ARRAY_REF (
392  kByteLookupTable,
393  ((crcRegister >> (Spec::kWidth - kBitsPerByte))
394  ^ static_cast<std::uint64_t> (messageByte))
395  & kByteMaskU8);
396  crcRegister &= mask;
397  }
398  LUMEX_CONSTEXPR_IF (Spec::kRefOut)
399  crcRegister = Reflect (crcRegister, Spec::kWidth) & mask;
400  }
401  // NOLINTEND(cppcoreguidelines-pro-bounds-pointer-arithmetic)
402 
403  crcRegister = (crcRegister ^ Spec::kXorOut) & mask;
404  return static_cast<typename Spec::ValueType> (crcRegister);
405 }
406 
407 template <typename Spec, typename Enable = void> struct crc_dispatch_t;
408 
409 template <typename Spec>
410 struct crc_dispatch_t<
411  Spec, typename std::enable_if<(Spec::kWidth < kBitsPerByte), void>::type>
412 {
413  static LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR typename Spec::ValueType
414  Run (std::uint8_t const *data, std::size_t size) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
415  {
416  return ComputeBitwise<Spec> (data, size);
417  }
418 };
419 
420 template <typename Spec>
421 struct crc_dispatch_t<
422  Spec, typename std::enable_if<(Spec::kWidth >= kBitsPerByte), void>::type>
423 {
424  static LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR typename Spec::ValueType
425  Run (std::uint8_t const *data, std::size_t size) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
426  {
427  return ComputeTableDriven<Spec> (data, size);
428  }
429 };
430 
431 template <typename Spec>
432 LUMEX_CRC_DETAIL_CONSTEXPR typename Spec::ValueType
433 Compute (std::uint8_t const *data, std::size_t size) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
434 {
435  return crc_dispatch_t<Spec>::Run (data, size);
436 }

2425 // NOLINTEND(readability-identifier-naming)
2426 
2432 template <typename Spec> class CrcParametric
2433 {
2434 public:
2435  using ValueType = typename Spec::ValueType;
2436 
2437  CrcParametric () = delete;
2438  CrcParametric (CrcParametric const &) = delete;
2439  CrcParametric (CrcParametric &&) = delete;
2440  CrcParametric &operator= (CrcParametric const &) = delete;
2441  CrcParametric &operator= (CrcParametric &&) = delete;
2442  ~CrcParametric () = default;
2443 
2448  LUMEX_ATTRIBUTE_NODISCARD (
2449  "CRC result is required for integrity verification.")
2450  static ValueType
2451  calculate (std::uint8_t const *data, std::size_t size) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
2452  {
2453  return Detail::Compute<Spec> (data, size);
2454  }
2455 
2456  LUMEX_ATTRIBUTE_NODISCARD (
2457  "CRC result is required for integrity verification.")
2458  static ValueType
2459  calculate (std::vector<std::uint8_t> const &data) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
2460  {
2461  if (data.empty ())
2462  return Detail::Compute<Spec> (nullptr, 0);
2463  return Detail::Compute<Spec> (data.data (), data.size ());
2464  }
2465 
2466 #if LUMEX_HAS_STD_SPAN
2467  LUMEX_ATTRIBUTE_NODISCARD (
2468  "CRC result is required for integrity verification.")
2469  static ValueType
2470  calculate (std::span<std::uint8_t const> data) LUMEX_NOEXCEPT
2471  {
2472  return Detail::Compute<Spec> (data.data (), data.size ());
2473  }
2474 #endif
2475 };
2476
